'filter-btn-active' : ''}`}\n                onClick={() => setPeriod(opt.key)}\n              >\n                {opt.label}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      <main>\n        <KpiCards summary={kpiSummary} />\n\n        <Charts\n          data={filteredData}\n          activeMetric={activeMetric}\n          onMetricChange={setActiveMetric}\n        />\n\n        <section className=\"table-section\">\n          <div className=\"table-card\">\n            <div className=\"table-header\">\n              <h2 className=\"chart-title\">月別詳細実績一覧</h2>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"table-responsive\">\n              <table className=\"data-table\">\n                <thead>\n                  <tr>\n                    <th className=\"text-center\">月度</th>\n                    <th className=\"text-right\">売上高 (円)</th>\n                    <th className=\"text-right\">目標売上 (円)</th>\n                    <th className=\"text-center\">目標達成率</th>\n                    <th className=\"text-right\">客数 (人)</th>\n                    <th className=\"text-right\">客単価 (円)</th>\n                  </tr>\n                </thead>\n                <tbody>\n                  {filteredData.map((row) => {\n                    const achievementRate = Number(((row.sales / row.targetSales) * 100).toFixed(1));\n                    const isTargetMet = achievementRate >= 100;\n\n                    return (\n                      <tr key={row.month}>\n                        <td className=\"text-center\">{row.month}</td>\n                        <td className=\"text-right\">{row.sales.toLocaleString('ja-JP')}</td>\n                        <td className=\"text-right\">{row.targetSales.toLocaleString('ja-JP')}</td>\n                        <td className=\"text-center\">\n                          <span className={isTargetMet ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-down'}>\n                            {achievementRate}%\n                          </span>\n                        </td>\n                        <td className=\"text-right\">{row.customers.toLocaleString('ja-JP')}</td>\n                        <td className=\"text-right\">{row.averageSpend.toLocaleString('ja-JP')}</td>\n                      </tr>\n                    );\n                  })}\n                </tbody>\n              </table>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </section>\n      </main>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}

data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/KpiCards.tsx","content":"import { KpiSummary } from '../types';\n\ninterface KpiCardsProps {\n  summary?: KpiSummary;\n}\n\nconst formatNumber = (num: number | undefined | null): string => {\n  if (num == null || isNaN(num)) {\n    return '0';\n  }\n  return num.toLocaleString('ja-JP');\n};\n\nconst getRateStatus = (\n  rate: number | undefined | null,\n  positiveLabel: string,\n  negativeLabel: string\n) => {\n  const safeRate = rate == null || isNaN(rate) ? 0 : rate;\n\n  if (safeRate > 0) {\n    return {\n      badgeText: positiveLabel,\n      signText: `+${safeRate}%`,\n      symbol: '↑',\n      className: 'kpi-positive'\n    };\n  }\n\n  if (safeRate < 0) {\n    return {\n      badgeText: negativeLabel,\n      signText: `${safeRate}%`,\n      symbol: '↓',\n      className: 'kpi-negative'\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    badgeText: '横ばい',\n    signText: '±0%',\n    symbol: '→',\n    className: ''\n  };\n};\n\nconst KpiCards = ({ summary }: KpiCardsProps) => {\n  const safeSummary: KpiSummary = summary ?? {\n    totalSales: 0,\n    totalCustomers: 0,\n    averageSpend: 0,\n    salesGrowthRate: 0,\n    customerGrowthRate: 0,\n    spendGrowthRate: 0\n  };\n\n  const salesStatus = getRateStatus(safeSummary.salesGrowthRate, '好調', '要改善');\n  const customerStatus = getRateStatus(safeSummary.customerGrowthRate, '増加', '減少');\n  const spendStatus = getRateStatus(safeSummary.spendGrowthRate, '上昇', '下降');\n\n  const cards = [\n    {\n      title: '総売上高',\n      value: formatNumber(safeSummary.totalSales),\n      unit: '円',\n      status: salesStatus\n    },\n    {\n      title: '総客数',\n      value: formatNumber(safeSummary.totalCustomers),\n      unit: '人',\n      status: customerStatus\n    },\n    {\n      title: '平均客単価',\n      value: formatNumber(safeSummary.averageSpend),\n      unit: '円',\n      status: spendStatus\n    }\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"kpi-grid\">\n      {cards.map((card) => (\n        <div key={card.title} className=\"kpi-card\">\n          <div className=\"kpi-card-header\">\n            <span className=\"kpi-label\">{card.title}</span>\n            <span className=\"kpi-badge\">{card.status.badgeText}</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"kpi-value-row\">\n            <span className=\"kpi-value\">{card.value}</span>\n            <span className=\"kpi-unit\">{card.unit}</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className={`kpi-compare ${card.status.className}`.trim()}>\n            <span>前月比: {card.status.symbol} {card.status.signText}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default KpiCards;","language":"tsx"}

event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { PeriodFilter, ChartMetric } from './types';\nimport { mockMonthlyData, filterMonthlyData, calculateKpiSummary } from './mockData';\nimport KpiCards from './components/KpiCards';\nimport Charts from './components/Charts';\nimport './index.css';\n\ntype TableSortKey = 'month' | 'sales' | 'targetSales' | 'achievementRate' | 'customers' | 'averageSpend';\ntype SortOrder = 'asc' | 'desc';\n\ninterface TableSortConfig {\n  key: TableSortKey;\n  order: SortOrder;\n}\n\nconst calculateAchievementRate = (sales: number, targetSales: number): number => {\n  if (!targetSales || targetSales === 0) return 0;\n  return Number(((sales / targetSales) * 100).toFixed(1));\n};\n\nconst parseMonthNumber = (monthStr: string): number => {\n  const match = monthStr.match(/\\d+/);\n  return match ? parseInt(match[0], 10) : 0;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<PeriodFilter>('all');\n  const [activeMetric, setActiveMetric] = useState<ChartMetric>('all');\n  const [sortConfig, setSortConfig] = useState<TableSortConfig>({ key: 'month', order: 'asc' });\n\n  const filteredData = useMemo(() => {\n    return filterMonthlyData(mockMonthlyData, period);\n  }, [period]);\n\n  const kpiSummary = useMemo(() => {\n    return calculateKpiSummary(filteredData);\n  }, [filteredData]);\n\n  const tableRows = useMemo(() => {\n    const list = filteredData.map((row) => ({\n      ...row,\n      achievementRate: calculateAchievementRate(row.sales, row.targetSales)\n    }));\n\n    list.sort((a, b) => {\n      let comparison = 0;\n      if (sortConfig.key === 'month') {\n        comparison = parseMonthNumber(a.month) - parseMonthNumber(b.month);\n      } else {\n        comparison = a[sortConfig.key] - b[sortConfig.key];\n      }\n      return sortConfig.order === 'asc' ? comparison : -comparison;\n    });\n\n    return list;\n  }, [filteredData, sortConfig]);\n\n  const tableSummary = useMemo(() => {\n    const totalSales = filteredData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (cur.sales || 0), 0);\n    const totalTarget = filteredData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (cur.targetSales || 0), 0);\n    const totalCustomers = filteredData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (cur.customers || 0), 0);\n    const overallAchievement = calculateAchievementRate(totalSales, totalTarget);\n    const weightedAverageSpend = totalCustomers > 0 ? Math.round(totalSales / totalCustomers) : 0;\n\n    return {\n      totalSales,\n      totalTarget,\n      overallAchievement,\n      totalCustomers,\n      weightedAverageSpend\n    };\n  }, [filteredData]);\n\n  const handleSort = (key: TableSortKey) => {\n    setSortConfig((prev) => {\n      if (prev.key === key) {\n        return {\n          key,\n          order: prev.order === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc'\n        };\n      }\n      return {\n        key,\n        order: 'desc'\n      };\n    });\n  };\n\n  const getSortIcon = (key: TableSortKey) => {\n    if (sortConfig.key !== key) return ' ↕';\n    return sortConfig.order === 'asc' ? 'filter-btn-active' : ''}`}\n                onClick={() => setPeriod(opt.key)}\n              >\n                {opt.label}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      <main>\n        <KpiCards summary={kpiSummary} />\n\n        <Charts\n          data={filteredData}\n          activeMetric={activeMetric}\n          onMetricChange={setActiveMetric}\n        />\n\n        <section className=\"table-section\">\n          <div className=\"table-card\">\n            <div className=\"table-header\">\n              <h2 0 : rate;\n\n  if (safeRate > 0) {\n    return {\n      badgeText: positiveLabel,\n      signText: `+${safeRate}%`,\n      symbol: '↑',\n      className: 'kpi-positive'\n    };\n  }\n\n  if (safeRate < 0) {\n    return {\n      badgeText: negativeLabel,\n      signText: `${safeRate}%`,\n      symbol: '↓',\n      className: 'kpi-negative'\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    badgeText: '横ばい',\n    signText: '±0%',\n    symbol: '→',\n    className: ''\n  };\n};\n\nconst KpiCards = ({ summary }: KpiCardsProps) => {\n  const safeSummary: KpiSummary = summary ?? {\n    totalSales: 0,\n    totalCustomers: 0,\n    averageSpend: 0,\n    salesGrowthRate: 0,\n    customerGrowthRate: 0,\n    spendGrowthRate: 0\n  };\n\n  const salesStatus = getRateStatus(safeSummary.salesGrowthRate, '好調', '要改善');\n  const customerStatus = getRateStatus(safeSummary.customerGrowthRate, '増加', '減少');\n  const spendStatus = getRateStatus(safeSummary.spendGrowthRate, '上昇', '下降');\n\n  const cards = [\n    {\n      title: '総売上高',\n      value: formatNumber(safeSummary.totalSales),\n      unit: '円',\n      status: salesStatus\n    },\n    {\n      title: '総客数',\n      value: formatNumber(safeSummary.totalCustomers),\n      unit: '人',\n      status: customerStatus\n    },\n    {\n      title: '平均客単価',\n      value: formatNumber(safeSummary.averageSpend),\n      unit: '円',\n      status: spendStatus\n    }\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"kpi-grid\">\n      {cards.map((card) => (\n        <div key={card.title} className=\"kpi-card\">\n          <div className=\"kpi-card-header\">\n            <span className=\"kpi-label\">{card.title}</span>\n            <span className=\"kpi-badge\">{card.status.badgeText}</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"kpi-value-row\">\n            <span className=\"kpi-value\">{card.value}</span>\n            <span className=\"kpi-unit\">{card.unit}</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className={`kpi-compare ${card.status.className}`.trim()}>\n            <span>前月比: {card.status.symbol} {card.status.signText}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default KpiCards;","language":"tsx"}

event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { PeriodFilter, ChartMetric, TableSortKey, TableSortConfig } from './types';\nimport { mockMonthlyData, filterMonthlyData, calculateKpiSummary } from './mockData';\nimport KpiCards from './components/KpiCards';\nimport Charts from './components/Charts';\nimport './index.css';\n\nconst calculateAchievementRate = (sales: number, targetSales: number): number => {\n  if (!targetSales || targetSales === 0) return 0;\n  return Number(((sales / targetSales) * 100).toFixed(1));\n};\n\nconst parseMonthNumber = (monthStr: string): number => {\n  const match = monthStr.match(/\\d+/);\n  return match ? parseInt(match[0], 10) : 0;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<PeriodFilter>('all');\n  const [activeMetric, setActiveMetric] = useState<ChartMetric>('all');\n  const [sortConfig, setSortConfig] = useState<TableSortConfig>({ key: 'month', order: 'asc' });\n\n  const filteredData = useMemo(() => {\n    return filterMonthlyData(mockMonthlyData, period);\n  }, [period]);\n\n  const kpiSummary = useMemo(() => {\n    return calculateKpiSummary(filteredData);\n  }, [filteredData]);\n\n  const tableRows = useMemo(() => {\n    const list = filteredData.map((row) => ({\n      ...row,\n      achievementRate: calculateAchievementRate(row.sales, row.targetSales)\n    }));\n\n    list.sort((a, b) => {\n      let comparison = 0;\n      if (sortConfig.key === 'month') {\n        comparison = parseMonthNumber(a.month) - parseMonthNumber(b.month);\n      } else {\n        comparison = a[sortConfig.key] - b[sortConfig.key];\n      }\n      return sortConfig.order === 'asc' ? comparison : -comparison;\n    });\n\n    return list;\n  }, [filteredData, sortConfig]);\n\n  const tableSummary = useMemo(() => {\n    const totalSales = filteredData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (cur.sales || 0), 0);\n    const totalTarget = filteredData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (cur.targetSales || 0), 0);\n    const totalCustomers = filteredData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (cur.customers || 0), 0);\n    const overallAchievement = calculateAchievementRate(totalSales, totalTarget);\n    const weightedAverageSpend = totalCustomers > 0 ? Math.round(totalSales / totalCustomers) : 0;\n\n    return {\n      totalSales,\n      totalTarget,\n      overallAchievement,\n      totalCustomers,\n      weightedAverageSpend\n    };\n  }, [filteredData]);\n\n  const handleSort = (key: TableSortKey) => {\n    setSortConfig((prev) => {\n      if (prev.key === key) {\n        return {\n          key,\n          order: prev.order === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc'\n        };\n      }\n      return {\n        key,\n        order: 'desc'\n      };\n    });\n  };\n\n  const getSortIcon = (key: TableSortKey) => {\n    if (sortConfig.key !== key) return ' ↕';\n    return sortConfig.order === 'asc' ? ' ↑' : ' ↓';\n  };\n\n  const periodOptions: { key: PeriodFilter; label: string }[] = [\n    { key: 'all', label: '通期（1月〜12月）' },\n    { key: 'first-half', label: '上半期（1月〜6月）' },\n    { key: 'second-half', label: '下半期（7月〜12月）' },\n    { key: 'recent-3m', label: '直近3ヶ月' }\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"dashboard-container\">\n      <header className=\"dashboard-header\">\n        <div className=\"header-title-group\">\n          <h1 className=\"header-title\">店舗売上・実績分析ダッシュボード</h1>\n          <p className=\"header-subtitle\">\n            売上高、客数、客単価の推移と目標達成状況を可視化・分析します\n          </p>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"filter-bar\">\n          <div className=\"filter-group\">\n            {periodOptions.map((opt) => (\n              <button\n                key={opt.key}\n                type=\"button\"\n                className={`filter-btn ${period === opt.key ? 'filter-btn-active' : ''}`}\n                onClick={() => setPeriod(opt.key)}\n              >\n                {opt.label}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      <main>\n        <KpiCards summary={kpiSummary} />\n\n        <Charts\n          data={filteredData}\n          activeMetric={activeMetric}\n          onMetricChange={setActiveMetric}\n        />\n\n        <section className=\"table-section\">\n          <div className=\"table-card\">\n            <div className=\"table-header\">\n              <h2 className=\"chart-title\">月別詳細実績一覧</h2>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"table-responsive\">\n              <table className=\"data-table\">\n                <thead>\n                  <tr>\n                    <th\n                      className=\"text-center\"\n                      onClick={() => handleSort('month')}\n                      style={{ cursor: 'pointer', userSelect: 'none' }}\n                      title=\"月度で並び替え\"\n                    >\n                      月度{getSortIcon('month')}\n                    </th>\n                    <th\n                      className=\"text-right\"\n                      onClick={() => handleSort('sales')}\n                      style={{ cursor: 'pointer', userSelect: 'none' }}\n                      title=\"売上高で並び替え\"\n                    >\n                      売上高 (円){getSortIcon('sales')}\n                    </th>\n                    <th\n                      className=\"text-right\"\n                      onClick={() => handleSort('targetSales')}\n                      style={{ cursor: 'pointer', userSelect: 'none' }}\n                      title=\"目標売上で並び替え\"\n                    >\n                      目標売上 (円){getSortIcon('targetSales')}\n                    </th>\n                    <th\n                      className=\"text-center\"\n                      onClick={() => handleSort('achievementRate')}\n                      style={{ cursor: 'pointer', userSelect: 'none' }}\n                      title=\"目標達成率で並び替え\"\n                    >\n                      目標達成率{getSortIcon('achievementRate')}\n                    </th>\n                    <th\n                      className=\"text-right\"\n                      onClick={() => handleSort('customers')}\n                      style={{ cursor: 'pointer', userSelect: 'none' }}\n                      title=\"客数で並び替え\"\n                    >\n                      客数 (人){getSortIcon('customers')}\n                    </th>\n                    <th\n                      className=\"text-right\"\n                      onClick={() => handleSort('averageSpend')}\n
